Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any graph module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 66. The projective resolution has length at most 645 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_66(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 66. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^645(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on graph structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 968. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 847 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by graph generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 968, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 847. QED.
